Load data\n    catalog = json.load(THERAPY_CATALOG_PATH.open(\"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n\n    # Flatten therapies from the catalog structure.\n    all_therapies = []\n    for service_line in catalog.get(\"service_lines\", []):\n        for t in service_line.get(\"therapies\", []):\n            all_therapies.append(t)\n\n    # 2b. Filter in-scope therapies\n    in_scope = [t for t in all_therapies if t.get(\"include_in_review\") is True]\n\n    code_to_therapy = {t[\"therapy_code\"]: t for t in in_scope}\n\n    label_to_code = {}\n    collisions = []\n    for t in in_scope:\n        code = t[\"therapy_code\"]\n        labels = [t.get(\"therapy_name\", \"\")]\n        aliases = t.get(\"aliases\")\n        if isinstance(aliases, list):\n            labels.extend(aliases)\n        for lab in labels:\n            key = norm_label(lab)\n            if not key:\n                continue\n            if key in label_to_code and label_to_code[key] != code:\n                collisions.append((key, label_to_code[key], code))\n                # Keep the first mapping to avoid non-determinism.\n                continue\n            label_to_code[key] = code\n\n    # Bag supply costs lookup by bag_size_ml\n    bag_supply_cost_by_ml = {}\n    with BAG_SUPPLY_COST_PATH.open(\"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\", newline=\"\") as f:\n        reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n        for row in reader:\n            bag_ml = int(str(row.get(\"bag_size_ml\")).strip())\n            cost = d(row.get(\"bag_supply_cost_usd\"))\n            bag_supply_cost_by_ml[bag_ml] = cost\n\n    # 2c. Compute per-therapy financials\n    DAYS_7 = 7\n    DELIVERIES_7 = 52\n    DAYS_14 = 14\n    DELIVERIES_14 = 26\n\n    def compute_for_model(\n        drug_cost_per_1000_mg_usd: Decimal,\n        dose_mg_per_day: Decimal,\n        bag_supply_cost_usd: Decimal,\n        payment_per_delivery_per_patient_usd: Decimal,\n        active_patients: int,\n        days_per_delivery: int,\n        deliveries_per_year: int,\n    ):\n        ap = Decimal(active_patients)\n        dppd = Decimal(days_per_delivery)\n        dpy = Decimal(deliveries_per_year)\n\n        annual_drug_cost = (\n            drug_cost_per_1000_mg_usd * ap * dose_mg_per_day * dppd * dpy / Decimal(\"1000\")\n        )\n        annual_supply_cost = bag_supply_cost_usd * ap * dpy\n        annual_revenue = payment_per_delivery_per_patient_usd * ap * dpy\n        annual_margin = annual_revenue - annual_drug_cost - annual_supply_cost\n\n        return {\n            \"annual_drug_cost_usd\": r2(annual_drug_cost),\n            \"annual_supply_cost_usd\": r2(annual_supply_cost),\n            \"annual_revenue_usd\": r2(annual_revenue),\n            \"annual_margin_usd\": r2(annual_margin),\n        }\n\n    therapies_out = []\n    for code in sorted(code_to_therapy.keys()):\n        t = code_to_therapy[code]\n        therapy_name = t.get(\"therapy_name\")\n        bag_size_ml = int(t.get(\"bag_size_ml\"))\n\n        drug_cost_per_1000 = d(t.get(\"drug_cost_per_1000_mg_usd\"))\n        dose_mg_per_day = d(t.get(\"dose_mg_per_day\"))\n\n        bag_supply_cost_usd = bag_supply_cost_by_ml.get(bag_size_ml, Decimal(\"0\"))\n        payment = payment_by_code.get(code, Decimal(\"0\"))\n        active_patients = int(active_patients_by_code.get(code, 0))\n\n        m7 = compute_for_model(\n            drug_cost_per_1000,\n            dose_mg_per_day,\n            bag_supply_cost_usd,\n            payment,\n            active_patients,\n            DAYS_7,\n            DELIVERIES_7,\n        )\n        m14 = compute_for_model(\n            drug_cost_per_1000,\n            dose_mg_per_day,\n            bag_supply_cost_usd,\n            payment,\n            active_patients,\n            DAYS_14,\n            DELIVERIES_14,\n        )\n\n        margin_diff = r2(m14[\"annual_margin_usd\"] - Compute totals\n    total_7 = r2(sum((d(t[\"annual_margin_7_day_usd\"]) for t in therapies_out), Decimal(\"0\")))\n    total_14 = r2(sum((d(t[\"annual_margin_14_day_usd\"]) for t in therapies_out), Decimal(\"0\")))\n    total_diff = r2(\n        sum((d(t[\"annual_margin_difference_14_minus_7_usd\"]) for t in therapies_out), Decimal(\"0\"))\n    )\n    absolute_diff = r2(abs(total_diff))\n\n    totals_out = {\n        \"total_annual_margin_7_day_usd\": float(total_7),\n        \"total_annual_margin_14_day_usd\": float(total_14),\n        \"total_annual_margin_difference_14_minus_7_usd\": float(total_diff),\n        \"absolute_total_margin_difference_usd\": float(absolute_diff),\n    }\n\n    # 2g.
